Transcripts of “What It Means To Become A Shepherd” by Dag Heward-Mills (Flow Service). Presented verbatim — paragraph breaks are added for readability only; the words are unchanged. Fully offline — no internet and no scripts required. Parts 4 & 6 were transcribed from their audio; the rest from YouTube captions. One video in the playlist is private, so it is omitted.
